> Verified against: App 3.24.3 · Date: 2026-10-19 · Confidence: asm-traced · Page type: audit table · Owner page: this page

# Audit: BLE_COMMAND_MAP.md (Smi sweep)

Every integer in `BLE_COMMAND_MAP.md` that could have come from a Blutter immediate, checked against the asm. Method and verdict meanings: [../../METHODOLOGY.md](../../METHODOLOGY.md) (Smi rule). Asm roots: 3.24.3 `v3.24.3/blutter_output/asm`, 3.18.0 `blutter_output/asm`; `pp.txt` entries are pool objects and are already untagged (pool `List<int>(2)` opcode lists and `Obj!<Enum>` values are real). Addresses are `file.dart @0xADDR` relative to the asm root (`charging/ble/device/...` paths abbreviated where the table names the file in an earlier cell). The document itself is left unchanged except for the banner under its title.

Doc line numbers refer to the file BEFORE the banner was added (commit f687ec1); in the current file add 2.

Verdicts: `OK`, `WRONG` (real value in the note), `NOT-FROM-ASM` (string, upstream, device test, example or prose count), `UNVERIFIABLE` (site not found; search listed), `CHANGED-IN-3.24.3` (doc matches 3.18.0 but not 3.24.3). Byte offsets (`field_XXX`, `0xNNN`, `r17 = N`) are real byte offsets printed by Blutter and are not Smi-affected; they are checked only for presence in the cited parser (per-tag where the parser reads each tag inline, function-wide otherwise) and flagged `CHANGED-IN-3.24.3` when they moved. Verdict suffixes: `(tag only; binding not traced)` = the tag number is an `indexString` argument but the doc field / type binding was not verified; `(slot-traced)` = the offset was attributed to the tag by following the frame slot of its `indexString` result (heuristic, used for parsers such as A17X8 that fetch all tags first); `(function-wide)` = presence in the function only. WRONG rows whose note starts with `not Smi:` are semantic or arithmetic corrections (pairing, counts, layouts), not Smi decoding errors; they stay because the numbers were wrong.

Rows: 653. CHANGED-IN-3.24.3 175, NOT-FROM-ASM 97, OK 264, UNVERIFIABLE 37, WRONG 80.
